The guard is kept (ruling BC6H-C6): it removes NaN-dependent behaviour, and the cost if the call is wrong is about 0.5 % at Basic and slower.
- **LT5 Q6 skip-retry (patch 0006).** `bc6h_peak_retry` (VeryslowPeak, once per guarded block) sets a per-lane `lifted` flag when `lo > block[p * 16 + k]` for any channel (compared before the `max`, which is unchanged) and runs `bc6h_bounds`, the second `bc6h_search_slow` and the keep-the-better restore only where it is set (the gang skips the search when no lane lifted). An unlifted block would repeat the identical search from `best_err = 1e99`, and the retry keeps its own result on a tie, which is the same data. Task 2's count says the lift changes no channel in 211 of the 256 guarded `hdr_real` blocks. BC6H bytes do not change: `cargo test -p encoders-sys -p sdc` passes with no pin edited on SSE2 and AVX2, the `bc6h` binary with `SDC_TEST_AVX512=1` passes, `encode_versions` and `golden` pass with `identity.json` unchanged, the 48 and 72 strip hashes equal the Task 4 strips, and `bc6h_quality run` on SSE2, AVX2 and AVX-512 compared with the Task 4 CSVs says 18/18 and 4/4 identical at all six levels. Mutation (`if (!lifted)`): `the_peak_mode_is_byte_identical_to_the_recorded_objects` (RECORDED_PEAK) and `peak_mode_keeps_a_blue_zero_sun` fail on SSE2/AVX2 and with `SDC_TEST_AVX512=1`, and the VeryslowPeak strip hash of `a` moves on SSE2 and AVX2. VM time (AVX2 under Wine, one thread, 22 images, median of 5 alternating rounds, none discarded, busy-procs 0 throughout), median seconds Task 4 guard / skip-retry: Veryfast 0.08 / 0.08, Fast 0.60 / 0.59, Basic 1.50 / 1.49, Slow 6.60 / 6.59, Veryslow 14.21 / 14.18, VeryslowPeak 14.29 / 14.20, total 37.28 / 37.13. Ratios: 0.988, 0.987, 0.997, 0.998, 0.998, 0.994 and 0.996 in total. Spread (max/min), guard / skip: Veryfast 1.036 / 1.036, Fast 1.010 / 1.026, Basic 1.003 / 1.017, Slow 1.001 / 1.007, Veryslow 1.001 / 1.001, VeryslowPeak 1.005 / 1.002 (Task 1's A/A ratios: 0.989 at Veryfast, 1.000 to 1.001 elsewhere, VeryslowPeak 0.999). VeryslowPeak is faster beyond the noise: its five rounds are 14.180 to 14.206 s against 14.278 to 14.347 s, no overlap, and the peak pass now costs 0.02 s over Veryslow where it cost 0.08 s. Veryslow's 0.998 also has no overlapping rounds (14.174 to 14.182 against 14.206 to 14.219) although the retry does not run there, so about 0.2 % of it is not from this change by construction (code layout or alignment); Veryfast 0.988 is one-hundredth resolution (0.08 s), and Fast 0.987, Basic 0.997 and Slow 0.998 lie inside the exes' own spreads. No level is slower. Kept.
- **LT6 S1 rank-once (patch 0006).** `bc6h_enc_2p` ranked the 32 two-region partitions (key `part + bound * 64`, `partial_sort_list`) on every call, so each two-region mode repeated the same ranking of the same block: six times at Slow, Veryslow and VeryslowPeak, twice at Basic, once at Fast. That body is now `bc6h_rank_partitions` (LT7 renamed it `bc6h_block_cache`), unchanged except that it writes the new `state->part_list[32]`; it runs once at the end of `bc6h_setup` and once more in `bc6h_peak_retry`, inside `if (lifted)` right after `bc6h_bounds`, for the lifted lanes, in both places only when `fastSkipTreshold > 0` (Veryfast skips it). `bc6h_enc_2p` keeps `n = min(fastSkipTreshold, 32)` and searches the stored list. BC6H bytes do not change: the `cargo test -p encoders-sys -p sdc` run passes with no pin edited on SSE2 and AVX2, the `bc6h` binary with `SDC_TEST_AVX512=1` passes, `encode_versions` and `golden` pass with `identity.json` unchanged, the 48 and 72 strip hashes equal the LT5 strips, and `bc6h_quality run` on SSE2, AVX2 and AVX-512 compared with the LT5 CSVs says 18/18 and 4/4 identical at all six levels. Mutations: deleting the call in `bc6h_setup` (the ranking is then never written) fails `shipped_levels_are_byte_identical_to_the_recorded_objects`, `the_peak_mode_is_byte_identical_to_the_recorded_objects` and `the_plain_call_encodes_on_the_process_target` on SSE2/AVX2 and with `SDC_TEST_AVX512=1`, and moves 60 strip hashes. Deleting the call in `bc6h_peak_retry` (the lifted lane then searches the unlifted block's list) moves nothing: no pin, no strip hash and no `bc6h` test fails on any path, because VeryslowPeak runs `fastSkipTreshold` 32 and so searches all 32 partitions, and the list order matters only for exact ties between partitions; that call is kept as the byte-exact equivalent of the old per-call ranking, but no check here can see it. VM time (AVX2 under Wine, one thread, 22 images, median of 5 alternating rounds, none discarded, busy-procs 0 throughout, `bytes-id` equal in all ten runs), median seconds LT5 / S1: Veryfast 0.08 / 0.09, Fast 0.59 / 0.60, Basic 1.49 / 1.15, Slow 6.57 / 4.87, Veryslow 14.18 / 12.36, VeryslowPeak 14.21 / 12.41, total 37.13 / 31.47. Ratios: 1.024, 1.012, 0.773, 0.742, 0.872, 0.873 and 0.848 in total. Spread (max/min), LT5 / S1: Veryfast 1.012 / 1.024, Fast 1.012 / 1.046, Basic 1.006 / 1.006, Slow 1.006 / 1.008, Veryslow 1.003 / 1.006, VeryslowPeak 1.002 / 1.004 (Task 1's A/A ratios: 0.989 at Veryfast, 1.000 to 1.001 elsewhere). Basic, Slow, Veryslow and VeryslowPeak are faster far beyond the noise (the gain is 12.7 % to 25.8 %); Fast's 1.012 and Veryfast's 1.024 (0.08 to 0.09 s, one-hundredth resolution) lie inside the exes' own spreads, and Fast ranked at most once before (only gangs that entered a two-region mode) while every gang now ranks once in `bc6h_setup`, which can be slightly more work at Fast; its 1.012 is within the exes' spreads. The retry-path rank call has no check that can see it while VeryslowPeak's `fastSkipTreshold` is 32; a task that lowers it must add one. No level is slower. Kept.
- **LT7 S2 PCA once per block (patch 0006).** `block_segment_core` (the PCA fit) ran in `bc6h_enc_2p_part_fast` for each of the `n` ranked partitions' two regions in every two-region mode (2n fits x 6 modes per block at Slow, Veryslow and VeryslowPeak, x 2 at Basic, x 1 at Fast, and at Fast and Basic only gangs that entered a two-region mode paid) and in `bc6h_enc_1p` in each one-region mode (1 per mode at Veryfast to Basic, 4 at Slow and above); it depends on the block alone. The state gains `pca_ep_1p[8]` and `pca_ep_2p[32 * 16]` (rank k's two regions at `[k * 16 ..]`, about 32.5 KiB per gang on AVX-512: 520 floats x 16 lanes x 4 bytes = 33,280 bytes), and `bc6h_rank_partitions` is renamed `bc6h_block_cache`: it fills `pca_ep_1p` at every level (Veryfast included) and, when `fastSkipTreshold > 0`, ranks and fills `pca_ep_2p` for k < `n = bc6h_2p_depth(state) = min(fastSkipTreshold, 32)`; the new helper `bc6h_2p_depth` is also what `bc6h_enc_2p` reads, so writer and reader depth agree by construction. It runs once at the end of `bc6h_setup` and again for a lifted lane in `bc6h_peak_retry`'s `if (lifted)`, so `block_segment_core` runs 2n + 1 times per block (plus again after a lift) and every gang pays the fill. `bc6h_enc_2p_part_fast` (new `rank` argument, passed by `bc6h_enc_2p_list`) and `bc6h_enc_1p` copy the cached floats into their local `ep` before `ep_quant_dequant_bc6h`, which overwrites `ep`, so the cache stays immutable. The edit shifts later lines, so the kernel's live asserts name other lines and the objects differ for that reason too. BC6H bytes do not change: `cargo test -p encoders-sys -p sdc` passes with no pin edited on SSE2 and AVX2, the `bc6h` binary with `SDC_TEST_AVX512=1` passes, `encode_versions` and `golden` pass with `identity.json` unchanged, the 48 and 72 strip hashes equal the LT6 strips, and `bc6h_quality run` on SSE2, AVX2 and AVX-512 compared with the LT6 CSVs says 18/18 and 4/4 identical at all six levels. Mutations: (a) every rank copying rank 0's endpoints fails `shipped_levels_are_byte_identical_to_the_recorded_objects` and `the_peak_mode_is_byte_identical_to_the_recorded_objects` on SSE2/AVX2 and with `SDC_TEST_AVX512=1`, and moves 30 of the 48 SSE2 and AVX2 strip hashes; (b) deleting the call in `bc6h_peak_retry` (the lifted lane then keeps the unlifted block's list and endpoints; LT6 showed the stale list alone moves nothing, so the move is the endpoints) fails `the_peak_mode_is_byte_identical_to_the_recorded_objects` and `peak_mode_keeps_a_blue_zero_sun` on all three paths and moves the VeryslowPeak strip hash of `a` on SSE2 and AVX2. VM time (AVX2 under Wine, one thread, 22 images, median of 5 alternating rounds, none discarded, busy-procs 0 throughout, `bytes-id` equal in all ten runs), median seconds LT6 / S2: Veryfast 0.09 / 0.08, Fast 0.60 / 0.59, Basic 1.17 / 1.06, Slow 4.92 / 3.51, Veryslow 12.48 / 8.14, VeryslowPeak 12.50 / 8.15, total 31.71 / 21.55. Ratios: 0.976, 0.993, 0.908, 0.714, 0.652, 0.652 and 0.680 in total. Spread (max/min), LT6 / S2: Veryfast 1.036 / 1.084, Fast 1.019 / 1.024, Basic 1.021 / 1.017, Slow 1.005 / 1.012, Veryslow 1.005 / 1.029, VeryslowPeak 1.004 / 1.028 (Task 1's A/A ratios: 0.989 at Veryfast, 1.000 to 1.001 elsewhere). Basic, Slow, Veryslow and VeryslowPeak are faster beyond both exes' spreads (9.2 % to 34.8 %); Veryfast (0.08 and 0.09 s, one-hundredth resolution) and Fast (0.993 against spreads 1.019 and 1.024) are within noise. No level is slower. Kept.
- **LT8 Deferred qbounds (patch 0006).** At Veryfast, Fast and Basic each admitted `enc = false` selector in `bc6h_test_mode` built the endpoint bounds (`compute_qbounds` or `compute_qbounds2`, an 8-value `ep_quant_bc6h`) that the next admitted selector overwrote, and only the last reached `bc6h_enc_2p` or `bc6h_enc_1p`; the slow levels (all `enc = true`) are unchanged. The state gains `q_span` and `q_wide`: each branch of `bc6h_test_mode` still sets `epb` and `mode` and records its span and the widened channel (-1 for modes 10 to 13 and for 0, 1, 5, 9; `max_span_idx` for the others), and `bc6h_build_qbounds` calls `compute_qbounds2(state, q_span, q_wide)`, which with -1 doubles no channel and so equals `compute_qbounds`. It runs in `bc6h_test_mode` when `enc` is set and, in `CompressBlockBC6H_core`, right before `bc6h_enc_2p` and before `bc6h_enc_1p` (modes 9 and 10 admit at margin 0, so a selector always ran), once per block per encode instead of once per admitted selector. The kernel commit also rewords the stale `sdc patch 0004` comment on the `bc6h_2p_depth` call in `bc6h_enc_2p` to name the helper. BC6H bytes do not change, and the first form needed no second attempt: `cargo test -p encoders-sys -p sdc` passes with no pin edited on SSE2 and AVX2, the `bc6h` binary with `SDC_TEST_AVX512=1` passes, `encode_versions` and `golden` pass with `identity.json` unchanged, the 48 and 72 strip hashes equal the LT7 strips, and `bc6h_quality run` on SSE2, AVX2 and AVX-512 compared with the LT7 CSVs says 18/18 and 4/4 identical at all six levels. Mutation: dropping the `bc6h_build_qbounds` call before `bc6h_enc_1p` at Fast and Basic (kept at Veryfast, where no two-region encode runs and the bounds would be uninitialised: dropping it there too crashed the `bc6h` binary with a stack buffer overrun) fails `shipped_levels_are_byte_identical_to_the_recorded_objects` and `refinement_never_ends_worse_than_veryfast` on SSE2/AVX2 and with `SDC_TEST_AVX512=1`, and moves 4 Fast and 4 Basic hashes of the 48 SSE2 and AVX2 strip hashes. VM time (AVX2 under Wine, one thread, 22 images, alternating rounds, two runs; busy-procs counted at the start and end of each run), median seconds LT7 / LT8, run 1 (five rounds, the first LT8 round discarded for a competing process, so four kept for LT8): Veryfast 0.08 / 0.08, Fast 0.59 / 0.58, Basic 1.05 / 1.04, Slow 3.48 / 3.50, Veryslow 8.05 / 8.08, VeryslowPeak 8.06 / 8.09, total 21.32 / 21.38; ratios 0.982, 0.981, 0.993, 1.007, 1.004, 1.003 and 1.003 in total; spreads (max/min) LT7 / LT8: Veryfast 1.049 / 1.024, Fast 1.017 / 1.009, Basic 1.014 / 1.010, Slow 1.004 / 1.006, Veryslow 1.005 / 1.006, VeryslowPeak 1.002 / 1.006. Run 2 (another session used the VM, three rounds kept for LT7 and four for LT8): Fast 0.59 / 0.57 (ratio 0.969, spreads 1.027 / 1.009), Veryfast 0.994, Basic 0.989, Slow 1.000, Veryslow 1.000, VeryslowPeak 0.998, total 0.993, every spread at most 1.073. Fast is faster in both runs, by 1.9 % and 3.1 %, beyond both exes' spreads (1.017 and 1.009; 1.027 and 1.009), though only by a point in run 1; Veryfast (0.08 s, one-hundredth resolution) and Basic are within noise, and Slow, Veryslow and VeryslowPeak are within noise (Task 1's A/A ratios: 0.989 at Veryfast, 1.000 to 1.001 elsewhere). No level is slower beyond its spread. Kept.
